    \subsubsection{8.1.3 BERT fine-tuning}\label{bert-fine-tuning}

Now that we now about how BERT was pre-trained, we have to understand
how it will be able to classify my data. To do so, I will dive a bit
deeper into explaining how the transformer magic works and how I can
fine-tune it to my dataset.

The real power of BERT comes from the Transformer encoder, which
consists of multiple layers of self-attention and feed-forward networks.

Now that we know about how BERT was pre-trained, we have to understand
how it will be able to classify my data. To do so, I will dive a bit
deeper into explaining how the transformer magic works and how I can
fine-tune it to my dataset of text messages.

The real power of BERT comes from the Transformer encoder, which
consists of multiple layers of self-attention and feed-forward networks.
The self-attention mechanism is what allows BERT to truly understand
context by considering all words in a sequence simultaneously, rather
than just relying on local context like traditional RNNs. Given an input
sequence of tokens, each token is first embedded and then transformed
into three different vectors: a query \$ Q \$, a key \$ K \$, and a
value \$ V \$. These are computed using learned weight matrices:

\[
Q = XW_Q, \quad K = XW_K, \quad V = XW_V
\]

where \$ X \$ is the matrix of token embeddings, and \$ W\_Q, W\_K, W\_V
\$ are the learned projection matrices. The attention scores are
computed by taking the dot product of the query and key vectors, scaled
by the square root of the key dimension \$ d\_k \$, and applying a
softmax function:

\[
\text{Attention}(Q, K, V) = \text{softmax} \left( \frac{QK^T}{\sqrt{d_k}} \right) V
\]

This gives a weighted sum of the value vectors, allowing each token to
incorporate information from all other tokens in the sequence. To make
things even more powerful, BERT uses \textbf{multi-head attention},
where multiple attention mechanisms run in parallel, each learning
different contextual relationships. The outputs of these attention heads
are concatenated and passed through a linear transformation.

Once self-attention has enriched the token representations, they are fed
into a \textbf{position-wise feed-forward network}. This is essentially
a two-layer neural network with a non-linearity, applied independently
to each token:

\[
\text{FFN}(x) = \text{ReLU}(xW_1 + b_1) W_2 + b_2
\]

This helps introduce further transformation capacity, allowing BERT to
refine token representations. Layer normalization and residual
connections are applied throughout the network to stabilize training.

Now, let's talk about how BERT is able to tell whether a given message
was sent by me or Arthur. The answer lies in \textbf{transfer learning
and fine-tuning}. Rather than training an entire deep network from
scratch, I'm leveraging BERT's deep understanding of language---acquired
during pretraining---and adapting it to this very specific
classification task.

In my current setup, I am \textbf{freezing most of BERT's layers} and
\textbf{only training the classification head and the last layer}. That
means that most of the weights in the transformer encoder remain
untouched. Only the final linear layer that takes the \texttt{{[}CLS{]}}
token's representation and maps it to a prediction is trainable. This
approach drastically reduces training time and lowers the risk of
overfitting.

The classification process works like this: each input message is
tokenized and passed through the frozen BERT encoder. The output
includes the final hidden states for each token, but I'm only interested
in the representation of the \texttt{{[}CLS{]}} token, denoted \$
h\_\{\text{CLS}\} \$. This special token is designed to act as a summary
of the entire sequence. I feed this vector into a simple linear
classification layer with a softmax activation to get the predicted
class probabilities:

\[
P(y | X) = \text{softmax}(W h_{\text{CLS}} + b)
\]

Since I'm only updating the parameters of \$ W \$ and \$ b \$, training
is fast and efficient. For each batch, I pass the messages through the
model, extract the logits, and compute the \textbf{cross-entropy loss}
between the predicted and true labels:

\[
\mathcal{L} = -\sum_{i} y_i \log P(y_i | X)
\]

Backpropagation is applied only to the classification head. The rest of
BERT remains unchanged, acting as a powerful static feature extractor. I
use the \textbf{Adam optimizer} to update the trainable parameters,
along with a \textbf{learning rate scheduler} to stabilize convergence.

This part of the pipeline was summarized in Figure 5.

\subsubsection{8.1.4 BERT Classifier Training and
Validation}\label{bert-classifier-training-and-validation}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{15}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Optimizer}
\PY{n}{optimizer} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{AdamW}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{5e\PYZhy{}5}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Learning rate scheduler}
\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}training\PYZus{}steps} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 3 epochs}
\PY{n}{lr\PYZus{}scheduler} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{get\PYZus{}scheduler}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{name}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{linear}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}warmup\PYZus{}steps}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}training\PYZus{}steps}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}training\PYZus{}steps}
\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Loss function}
\PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{CrossEntropyLoss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{56}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{os}
\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{environ}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{TOKENIZERS\PYZus{}PARALLELISM}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{false}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Freeze all BERT parameters by default}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{False}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze the last encoder layer (layer 11)}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze LayerNorm and Pooler}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pooler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LayerNorm}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training Loop}
\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}

    \PY{n}{loop} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{loop}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{lr\PYZus{}scheduler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loop}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}postfix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | Training Loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{/}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \end{Verbatim}

    That's quite disappointing. In this experiment, the BERT classifier
performed poorly, performing only a bit better than random assignments.
When comparing the ROC curve of BERT with SVM and Naive Bayes, the
latter performed way better. This issue likely stems from multiple
factors. First, maybe BERT was not doing a great job with understanding
Portuguese. So, to improve the performance, I tried using the Portuguese
BERT (trained only on Portuguese text) instead of the Multi-language
BERT. However, it performed even worse (worse than random assignment),
showing a strong bias toward predicting only one class (``Arthur''),
which resulted in very low precision and recall for the other class
(``Mariana''). This experiment can be found on Appendix A.

Another possibility is that the BERT encoder was too frozen during
training, meaning the model could not adapt its deep language
representations to the specific task or dataset. While freezing the
encoder can reduce training time and prevent overfitting in small
datasets, it also limits the model's ability to learn task-specific
distinctions, especially critical when subtle linguistic patterns
differentiate the classes. Another option was, instead, that I should
have modified only the classification head. In Appendix B, I make an
experiment only changing the classification head, but I got very similar
results as this model.

At last, maybe 3 epochs might not have been enough to train BERT. So, I
tried running for 7 epochs in Appendix C, but, once again, the
performance was very similar to this model. Possibilities to try to
improve are running for even more epochs, using more data, and
unfreezing more of BERT layers.

\subsection{8.2 BERT as a Feature-based Transfer
Learning}\label{bert-as-a-feature-based-transfer-learning}

I did try to train more BERT last layers so that it can learn more from
my data, but because of the size of my dataset, the training was
estimated to take around 60 hours. So, for the sake of the time (and for
the sake of my laptop, which started to go wild with so many models
being run), I decided to use BERT only for the text pre-processing.
Another way to use BERT is as a feature-based transfer learning
technique that transform the raw Portuguese text messages into
contextual embeddings. This would be a step that substitutes the IF-IDF
approach. Instead of fine-tuning BERT for classification, as I have just
done in the section above, I will leverage its pre-trained knowledge to
extract the {[}CLS{]} token embedding for each message, which serves as
a fixed-length representation of the entire input. These embeddings,
capturing nuanced semantic and syntactic patterns, are then used as
input features for traditional machine learning models like SVM. This
approach enables me to benefit from BERT's deep language understanding
while maintaining flexibility and interpretability with classic
classifiers.

    Now that we have the three datasets, I can load BERT and embbed the
text.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{61}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{transformers}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{BertTokenizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{BertModel}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{torch}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Load pre\PYZhy{}trained BERT model and tokenizer}
\PY{n}{tokenizer} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{BertTokenizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pretrained}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{bert\PYZhy{}base\PYZhy{}multilingual\PYZhy{}cased}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{model} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{BertModel}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pretrained}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{bert\PYZhy{}base\PYZhy{}multilingual\PYZhy{}cased}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{get\PYZus{}bert\PYZus{}cls\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{texts}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{max\PYZus{}length}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}embeddings} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{n}{dataloader} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{DataLoader}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{texts}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Generating BERT embeddings}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Tokenize batch}
        \PY{n}{inputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tokenizer}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{list}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{return\PYZus{}tensors}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{pt}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{truncation}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{max\PYZus{}length}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{max\PYZus{}length}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{inputs}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{inputs}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Get embeddings}
        \PY{k}{with} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{no\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{cls\PYZus{}embeddings} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{last\PYZus{}hidden\PYZus{}state}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} [CLS] token}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Move to CPU and convert to numpy}
        \PY{n}{cls\PYZus{}embeddings} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cls\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{detach}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cpu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{numpy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{cls\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{array}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    Let's do some data exploration of the tokenized words.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{52}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Distribution of Token Lengths}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{legend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{99}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{preprocessing}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{LabelEncoder}

\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}texts} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{train\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}bert}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Message}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tolist}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}texts} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}bert}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Message}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tolist}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Generating BERT embeddings for training set...}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{get\PYZus{}bert\PYZus{}cls\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}texts}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Generating BERT embeddings for test set...}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{get\PYZus{}bert\PYZus{}cls\PYZus{}embeddings}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}texts}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Encode labels as integers (e.g., \PYZdq{}Mariana\PYZdq{} \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 1, \PYZdq{}Arthur\PYZdq{} \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 0)}
\PY{n}{label\PYZus{}encoder} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{LabelEncoder}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{label\PYZus{}encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}bert}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Sender}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{label\PYZus{}encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}df\PYZus{}bert}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Sender}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Uses same mapping}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{8.2.1 t-SNE: Exploratory Data
Analysis}\label{t-sne-exploratory-data-analysis}

Let's make a t-SNE plot of the data after the BERT embedding.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{75}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{manifold}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{TSNE}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{matplotlib}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{pyplot}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{plt}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{seaborn}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sns}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Apply t\PYZhy{}SNE to reduce BERT embeddings to 2D}
\PY{n}{tsne} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{TSNE}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}components}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{perplexity}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{30}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}iter}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1000}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}tsne} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tsne}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create scatterplot}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{figure}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{figsize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{8}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{6}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{sns}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{scatterplot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}tsne}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train\PYZus{}tsne}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hue}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{palette}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{coolwarm}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{alpha}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.7}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{t\PYZhy{}SNE Visualization of BERT Embeddings}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{t\PYZhy{}SNE Component 1}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{t\PYZhy{}SNE Component 2}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{legend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{title}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Sender}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{center}
    \adjustimage{max size={0.9\linewidth}{0.9\paperheight}}{pipeline2_files/pipeline2_71_0.png}
    \end{center}
    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    The t-SNE plot of the BERT embeddings shows that messages from both
senders are pretty mixed together, but there are also some clusters
where one sender is more dominant. This tells us that while the data
isn't perfectly linearly separable. Since the separation isn't clearly
linear, I'm planning to try out both SVM with a non-linear kernel (RBF).
This model is great at capturing complex patterns in high-dimensional
spaces by applying the kernel trick, which makes it a good fit for this
kind of data.

    Now, I can run the BERT embedded words into classic classifiers. Using
Naive Bayes would not make sense since it performs better as it assumes
words are independent, so the BERT idea of context would not be adding
much to the performance. So, I will try using SVM again. In the code
cell below, I will implement SVM similarly to how I did with the IF-IDF
embeddings. It is important to say that SVM from scikit learn uses rbf
kernels as default, so my model will be able to capture non-linear
behaviors.

\subsubsection{8.2.2 Training and Validation of SVM using Scikit
Learn}\label{training-and-validation-of-svm-using-scikit-learn}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{82}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{pipeline}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{Pipeline}
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{svm}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{SVC}
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{model\PYZus{}selection}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{StratifiedKFold}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{cross\PYZus{}val\PYZus{}score}
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{metrics}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{classification\PYZus{}report}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{accuracy\PYZus{}score}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{precision\PYZus{}recall\PYZus{}fscore\PYZus{}support}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{confusion\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{roc\PYZus{}curve}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{auc}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{matplotlib}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{pyplot}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{plt}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{seaborn}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sns}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Define pipeline: just the classifier (since X\PYZus{}train is already vectorized by BERT)}
\PY{n}{pipeline} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{Pipeline}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}
    \PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{clf}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{SVC}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{probability}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}  
\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Perform Stratified K\PYZhy{}Fold Cross\PYZhy{}Validation}
\PY{n}{kf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{StratifiedKFold}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}splits}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{shuffle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{cv\PYZus{}scores} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cross\PYZus{}val\PYZus{}score}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pipeline}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{cv}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{kf}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{scoring}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{accuracy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Print cross\PYZhy{}validation results}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Mean Accuracy (Cross\PYZhy{}Validation): }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{cv\PYZus{}scores}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Standard Deviation: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{cv\PYZus{}scores}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{std}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train model on full training set}
\PY{n}{pipeline}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Evaluate on test set}
\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}pred} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pipeline}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{predict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    Wow!

The SVM classifier, powered by BERT embeddings, delivered impressive
performance with a \textbf{test accuracy of 95.3\%}. The classification
report reveals a strong balance between precision and recall across both
classes. For class 0, the model achieved a \textbf{precision of 94\%}
and a \textbf{recall of 97\%}, indicating a high ability to correctly
identify instances of this class with minimal false negatives. Class 1
mirrored this performance with a \textbf{precision of 97\%} and a
\textbf{recall of 94\%}, showing it was equally effective at avoiding
false positives. The overall \textbf{F1-score of 0.9530} reflects the
model's robustness and consistency. These results suggest that the
combination of BERT embeddings and the SVM classifier forms a highly
reliable and accurate text classification pipeline.

\subsection{8.3 SVM Code From Scratch}\label{svm-code-from-scratch}

Can I create my own SVM code from scratch, that uses the kernel trick?
Will it yield similar results? I will try that in the code cell below.

    \subsubsection{8.3.1 Defining the class
MySVM}\label{defining-the-class-mysvm}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{132}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{numpy}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{np}
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{sklearn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{base}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{BaseEstimator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ClassifierMixin}

\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{MySVM}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{BaseEstimator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ClassifierMixin}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Custom Support Vector Machine classifier using the RBF (Gaussian) kernel.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{    This implementation uses the dual form of the SVM optimization problem and applies}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    the kernel trick to allow for non\PYZhy{}linear classification boundaries.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{C}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{1.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{gamma}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{epochs}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Initializes the SVM with regularization and kernel parameters.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Parameters:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} C: float, regularization parameter (controls margin vs. slack).}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} gamma: float, RBF kernel parameter (controls kernel spread).}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} epochs: int, number of passes over the training data.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{C} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{C}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{gamma} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{gamma}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{epochs}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{rbf\PYZus{}kernel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X2}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Computes the RBF (Gaussian) kernel between two matrices.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Parameters:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} X1: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples\PYZus{}1, n\PYZus{}features)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} X2: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples\PYZus{}2, n\PYZus{}features)}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} Kernel matrix of shape (n\PYZus{}samples\PYZus{}1, n\PYZus{}samples\PYZus{}2)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Efficient computation of pairwise squared Euclidean distances}
        \PY{n}{X1\PYZus{}sq} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X1} \PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{axis}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{reshape}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{X2\PYZus{}sq} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X2} \PY{o}{*}\PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{axis}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{reshape}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{dist\PYZus{}sq} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X1\PYZus{}sq} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{X2\PYZus{}sq} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X2}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{T}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Apply the RBF kernel function element\PYZhy{}wise}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{exp}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{gamma} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{dist\PYZus{}sq}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{fit}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Trains the SVM model using stochastic gradient ascent in the dual form.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Parameters:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} X: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples, n\PYZus{}features), training features}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} y: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples,), training labels in \PYZob{}0, 1\PYZcb{}}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} self: fitted model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{where}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{y} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert labels to \PYZob{}\PYZhy{}1, 1\PYZcb{}}
        \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{X}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zeros}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Lagrange multipliers (dual coefficients)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute the full kernel matrix for training data}
        \PY{n}{K} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{rbf\PYZus{}kernel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train using a simple gradient update on the dual form}
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Training SVM with RBF Kernel}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute the margin for sample i}
                \PY{n}{margin} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{K}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

                \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Update alpha if constraint is violated (hinge loss)}
                \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{margin} \PY{o}{\PYZlt{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
                    \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{C} \PY{o}{*} \PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{margin}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Identify support vectors (non\PYZhy{}zero alpha)}
        \PY{n}{sv} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{l+m+mf}{1e\PYZhy{}5}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{sv}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sv\PYZus{}X} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{X\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{sv}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sv\PYZus{}y} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}train}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{sv}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Support vectors: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{decision\PYZus{}function}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Computes the decision function values for input samples.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Parameters:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} X: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples, n\PYZus{}features)}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} Scores (real\PYZhy{}valued), where sign determines class}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute kernel between input and support vectors}
        \PY{n}{K} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{rbf\PYZus{}kernel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sv\PYZus{}X}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Weighted sum of kernel outputs with support vector labels and coefficients}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{K}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{alpha} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sv\PYZus{}y}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{predict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Predicts the binary class labels for input samples.}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Parameters:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} X: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples, n\PYZus{}features)}

\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZhy{} Predictions: ndarray of shape (n\PYZus{}samples,), values in \PYZob{}0, 1\PYZcb{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Apply threshold to decision function}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{where}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{decision\PYZus{}function}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{X}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Test Accuracy: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}accuracy}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute precision, recall, and F1\PYZhy{}score}
\PY{n}{precision}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{recall}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{f1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{precision\PYZus{}recall\PYZus{}fscore\PYZus{}support}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{y\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{y\PYZus{}pred}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{average}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{weighted}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Precision: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{precision}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, Recall: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{recall}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, F1\PYZhy{}score: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{f1}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Predicted}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Actual}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    Yay! It performs very similarly to the scikit learn SVM, with very high
accuracy, precision, recall, and f1-score! WHile scikit learn SVM has
test accuracy of 95\%, my own SVM has test accuracy of 92\%. This slight
difference might be because I am not performing cross-validation in
MySVM training, while I used it with the scikit learn model.

    \section{9. Text Generation}\label{text-generation}

\subsection{9.1 LSTM}\label{lstm}

Imagine you're teaching a robot to write by letting it read a ton of
your messages. At first, it doesn't understand a thing---it just sees a
long string of characters. So, we give it a little help: we convert each
character into a number using a custom dictionary. That way, the robot
can process your messages as a sequence of numbers instead of letters.
Then comes the fun part: we break this long sequence into chunks---like
giving the robot short quotes from your texts to study. Using an LSTM (a
type of neural network with memory), the robot learns patterns over
time. It figures out, for example, that ``Mariana said:'' is often
followed by something thoughtful, funny, or dramatic. During training,
the LSTM tries to guess the next character in each chunk, over and over,
adjusting itself each time it gets it wrong. Once it's trained, we give
it a little spark of inspiration (like the phrase ``Mariana said:''),
and it uses what it learned to dream up the rest---predicting one
character at a time, based on everything it's seen before. It's like
autocomplete on steroids, powered by vibes, probability, and a memory
that spans 100 characters back. Magic? Almost. Just some clever math and
a lot of repetition.

Alright, let's peek behind the curtain and see what's really going on
while the LSTM is learning to write like me. So first, we feed it
batches of text sequences, like little windows of 100 characters taken
from my messages. For each window, we want the model to learn to predict
the next character at every position. If the input is ``Mariana sai'',
we expect it to guess ``ariana said'', shifting everything one character
ahead.

Before anything hits the LSTM, each character (which we turned into an
integer) gets embedded. This means we look up a trainable vector for
each character in an embedding matrix. Think of this like turning raw
numbers into little clouds of meaning, placing each character in a rich,
multidimensional space. So the input becomes a sequence of vectors,
which we send into the LSTM.

Now here's the brainy part: the LSTM processes these vectors step by
step, one character at a time, using the magic of \textbf{gates}. Each
step involves computing:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Forget gate:} \$ f\_t = \sigma(W\_f x\_t + U\_f h\_\{t-1\} +
  b\_f) \$\\
\item
  \textbf{Input gate:} \$ i\_t = \sigma(W\_i x\_t + U\_i h\_\{t-1\} +
  b\_i) \$\\
\item
  \textbf{Candidate memory:} \$ \tilde{c}\emph{t = \tanh(W\_c x\_t +
  U\_c h}\{t-1\} + b\_c) \$\\
\item
  \textbf{Memory update:} \$ c\_t = f\_t \odot c\_\{t-1\} + i\_t
  \odot \tilde{c}\_t \$
\item
  \textbf{Output gate:} \$ o\_t = \sigma(W\_o x\_t + U\_o h\_\{t-1\} +
  b\_o) \$
\item
  \textbf{Hidden state:} \$ h\_t = o\_t \odot \tanh(c\_t) \$
\end{itemize}

So much squiggly math! But here's the vibe: the model decides what to
remember, what to forget, and what to update at every step using those
gates. It's like a smart notepad that's constantly being rewritten
depending on the latest info.

Once the LSTM has processed the whole sequence, it outputs a hidden
state for each timestep. We pass these through a fully connected layer
(aka a fancy matrix multiplication) to get \textbf{logits}, which are
like raw scores for each character in our vocabulary.

Now comes the \emph{learning} part. We compare these predicted logits to
the true next characters using \textbf{Cross Entropy Loss}:

\[
\mathcal{L} = -\sum_{i=1}^{N} \log P(y_i | x_i)
\] This loss measures how wrong the model was. Then, using
\textbf{backpropagation through time} (yes, time travel is involved!),
we update all the weights---embedding matrix, LSTM weights, fully
connected layer---using \textbf{gradient descent}.

This whole process happens thousands of times over every batch of data,
across multiple epochs, slowly nudging the model from total confusion to
``okay, I kinda get your style now.''

So by the end of training, the model isn't memorizing my messages, it's
internalized the structure, rhythm, and quirks of my text. That's why
when I say ``Mariana said:'', it doesn't just randomly mash letters, it
taps into its memory, crunches probabilities, and continues the sentence
with surprising fluency.

\subsection{9.3 Training and Testing
LSTM}\label{training-and-testing-lstm}

I will train the model only on my text messages, as I want it to learn
to text like myself.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{146}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{torch}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{torch}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{nn}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{nn}
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{torch}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{nn}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{functional}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{as}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{F}
\PY{k+kn}{from}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{torch}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{utils}\PY{n+nn}{.}\PY{n+nn}{data}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k+kn}{import} \PY{n}{Dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{DataLoader}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Prepare your text data}
\PY{n}{text} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}1\PYZus{}sample}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Message}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tolist}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} or any text column}
\PY{n}{chars} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{sorted}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{text}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{chars}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Mappings}
\PY{n}{char2idx} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{ch}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{idx} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{chars}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
\PY{n}{idx2char} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{ch} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{ch}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{idx} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{char2idx}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{items}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Encode full text}
\PY{n}{encoded\PYZus{}text} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{char2idx}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{c}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{text}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create Dataset}
\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{CharDataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{Dataset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{data}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}len\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}getitem\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{p}{(}
            \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tensor}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{:}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{o}{+}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
            \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tensor}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{o}{+}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{100}
\PY{n}{dataset} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{CharDataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{encoded\PYZus{}text}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{dataloader} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{DataLoader}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{shuffle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 3. LSTM Model}
\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{LSTMGenerator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Module}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{embedding\PYZus{}dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden\PYZus{}dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{embedding} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Embedding}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{embedding\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{lstm} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LSTM}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{embedding\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}first}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Linear}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{hidden\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{embedding}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{out}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{lstm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{out} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{out}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{out}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden}

\PY{n}{model} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{LSTMGenerator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{optimizer} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Adam}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.005}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{CrossEntropyLoss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 4. Training Loop}
\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{5}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{xb}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{yb} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{xb}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{view}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{vocab\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{yb}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{view}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, Loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.2f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} 5. Text Generation}
\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{generate\PYZus{}text}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{model}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{start\PYZus{}text}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{length}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{300}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{eval}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}seq} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tensor}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{char2idx}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{c}\PY{p}{]} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{c} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{start\PYZus{}text}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unsqueeze}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{generated} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{start\PYZus{}text}
    \PY{n}{hidden} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{None}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{length}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}seq}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n}{seq\PYZus{}length}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hidden}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{probs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{F}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{softmax}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{output}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{detach}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{idx} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{multinomial}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{probs}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{generated} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{idx2char}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{]}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}seq} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cat}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}seq}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tensor}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{generated}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{9.3 Interpreting Early LSTM Text Generation
Results}\label{interpreting-early-lstm-text-generation-results}

The output generated by the LSTM model represents a promising early
stage of learning. Even though the generated text is not yet coherent or
grammatically correct, there are several signs that indicate the model
is starting to capture the structure and style of the dataset.

Firstly, the model successfully mimics the conversational tone found in
my text messages. Expressions like ``tamo baby'', ``kskkakak'', and
``pra gente'', as well as the inclusion of emojis (😍🙌🏼🤡), demonstrate
that the model is learning to replicate informal, real-life
communication patterns. This is particularly impressive given that
emojis are non-standard characters and their appropriate placement
suggests the model is learning contextual usage.

Secondly, the model shows it has started to grasp morphological patterns
in Portuguese. Most of the words exists, but it also generates
non-existent but plausible words like ``reunizora'', ``Maquitico'', and
``namorantiga'', which resemble real words in structure. This suggests
the LSTM is picking up on how words are typically formed, even if it
hasn't fully learned their meanings.

In addition, the model handles punctuation and spacing in a fairly
natural way. Phrases such as ``Mariana disse: pronta veio Vc\ldots{}''
indicate that it understands how tokens are usually sequenced in
conversational text. This reflects the model's ability to learn token
transitions and the rhythm of informal language.

However, as expected for a model in its early stages, there are
limitations. The generated text lacks semantic coherence. Ideas shift
abruptly, and the model doesn't maintain logical sentence flow. This is
typical for LSTMs that have a limited memory window and no explicit
understanding of syntax or meaning. Grammar and sentence construction
are also inconsistent, which is normal when training on unstructured,
informal data without grammatical labels.

Overall, these outputs show that the model has learned the ``texture''
and stylistic patterns of the dataset, even if it hasn't yet mastered
coherent text generation. This is a critical foundation.

In short, this is exactly what one should expect from an early-stage
LSTM: it's speaking the right language, just not quite fluently yet.

    \subsection{10. Possible improvements}\label{possible-improvements}

I am curious to explore further why my BERT classifier did not perform
as expected. To improve, I will make sure to have a balanced train
dataset (my overall dataset is balanced, but the train-test split might
make it unbalanced), and also try to run it for more epochs and
unfreezing more layers. I also want to try lazypredict to see if other
models (beside SVM) would give better accuracy.

I'm considering translating everything to English. Maybe the portuguese
BERT is not as good as the English BERT, which could give my model a
little performance boost. It's worth testing out!

Finally, for the text generation, the model can be trained for more
epochs, and techniques like adjusting sampling temperature, expanding
model size (e.g., more LSTM layers or units), and further cleaning the
dataset can be used to enhance its performance.

    \section{12. AI statement}\label{ai-statement}

I used Grammarly to help me with grammatical and spelling mistakes. In
the previous assignment, I used ChatGPT to help me use the space and
wordcloud library, to apply TF-IDF to my data sets, and to help me
modify my Naive Bayes classifier to the SVM. For this assignment, I used
ChatGPT to help me translate code I found in online guides of how to
apply BERT to my context, and the same for LSTM. When my BERT model was
giving me bad results, I asked ChatGPT to brainstorm possible reasons
for the bad performance.

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
BertForSequenceClassification(
  (bert): BertModel(
    (embeddings): BertEmbeddings(
      (word\_embeddings): Embedding(29794, 768, padding\_idx=0)
      (position\_embeddings): Embedding(512, 768)
      (token\_type\_embeddings): Embedding(2, 768)
      (LayerNorm): LayerNorm((768,), eps=1e-12, elementwise\_affine=True)
      (dropout): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
    )
    (encoder): BertEncoder(
      (layer): ModuleList(
        (0-11): 12 x BertLayer(
          (attention): BertAttention(
            (self): BertSdpaSelfAttention(
              (query): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=768, bias=True)
              (key): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=768, bias=True)
              (value): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=768, bias=True)
              (dropout): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
            )
            (output): BertSelfOutput(
              (dense): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=768, bias=True)
              (LayerNorm): LayerNorm((768,), eps=1e-12, elementwise\_affine=True)
              (dropout): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
            )
          )
          (intermediate): BertIntermediate(
            (dense): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=3072, bias=True)
            (intermediate\_act\_fn): GELUActivation()
          )
          (output): BertOutput(
            (dense): Linear(in\_features=3072, out\_features=768, bias=True)
            (LayerNorm): LayerNorm((768,), eps=1e-12, elementwise\_affine=True)
            (dropout): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
          )
        )
      )
    )
    (pooler): BertPooler(
      (dense): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=768, bias=True)
      (activation): Tanh()
    )
  )
  (dropout): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
  (classifier): Linear(in\_features=768, out\_features=2, bias=True)
)
\end{Verbatim}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{os}
\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{environ}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{TOKENIZERS\PYZus{}PARALLELISM}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{false}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Freeze all BERT parameters by default}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{False}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze the last encoder layer (layer 11)}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze LayerNorm and Pooler}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pooler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LayerNorm}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training Loop}
\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}

    \PY{n}{loop} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{loop}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{lr\PYZus{}scheduler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loop}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}postfix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | Training Loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{/}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\PY{k}{with} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{no\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{probs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{softmax}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert logits to probabilities}

        \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}labels}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{labels}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cpu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{numpy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

\prompt{In}{incolor}{46}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{os}
\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{environ}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{TOKENIZERS\PYZus{}PARALLELISM}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{false}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Freeze BERT encoder (everything except classification head)}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{False}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training Loop}
\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{loop}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{lr\PYZus{}scheduler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loop}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}postfix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | Training Loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{/}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{color}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{gray}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{linestyle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Random guess line}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlim}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{59}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k+kn}{import}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nn}{os}
\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{environ}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{TOKENIZERS\PYZus{}PARALLELISM}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{false}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Freeze all BERT parameters by default}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{False}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze the last encoder layer (layer 11)}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Unfreeze LayerNorm and Pooler}
\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pooler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{param} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bert}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{encoder}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{layer}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{11}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LayerNorm}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{param}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{requires\PYZus{}grad} \PY{o}{=} \PY{k+kc}{True}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training Loop}
\PY{n}{epochs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}

    \PY{n}{loop} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epochs}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{loop}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss\PYZus{}fn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{outputs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{logits}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{lr\PYZus{}scheduler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{loop}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{set\PYZus{}postfix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | Training Loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{/}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Set model to evaluation mode}

\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}preds}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}probs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{k}{with} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{no\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

        \PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{batch}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{outputs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{bert\PYZus{}model}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{input\PYZus{}ids}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{attention\PYZus{}mask}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{preds} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{argmax}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{probs}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}probs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{probs}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cpu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{numpy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Get probability for class 1}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Classification Report}

\PY{n}{report} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{classification\PYZus{}report}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}preds}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{target\PYZus{}names}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{class\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{📌 Classification Report:}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{report}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Confusion Matrix}
\PY{n}{conf\PYZus{}matrix} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{confusion\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}preds}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Plot Confusion Matrix}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{figure}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{figsize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{6}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{sns}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{heatmap}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{conf\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{annot}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fmt}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{d}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{cmap}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Blues}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{xticklabels}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{class\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{yticklabels}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{class\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Predicted Label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{True Label}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Confusion Matrix}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute \PYZam{} Plot ROC Curve}
\PY{n}{fpr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{tpr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{roc\PYZus{}curve}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{all\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}probs}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Use probability scores for ROC}
\PY{n}{roc\PYZus{}auc} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{auc}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fpr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{tpr}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{figure}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{figsize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{6}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fpr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{tpr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{color}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{blue}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lw}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{label}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ROC curve (AUC = }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{roc\PYZus{}auc}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.2f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{color}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{gray}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{linestyle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZhy{}\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Random guess line}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlim}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylim}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.05}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{False Positive Rate}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{True Positive Rate}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) Curve}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{legend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{lower right}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create a summary DataFrame with key metrics}
\PY{n}{metrics\PYZus{}df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{DataFrame}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{\PYZob{}}

\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{p}{)}
